`${badgeStyle.accent}15` }}\n      >\n        <div\n          className={`${\n            isModal ? \'w-24 h-24 sm:w-28 sm:h-28 text-3xl sm:text-4xl\' : \'w-20 h-20 sm:w-24 sm:h-24 text-2xl }}\n        >\n          {member.name\n            .split(\' \')\n            .filter((w) => !w.startsWith(\'Prof\') && !w.startsWith(\'Ir\') && !w.startsWith(\'M.\') && !w.startsWith(\'Ph.\') &&        {member.role}\n        </span>\n        {member.division && (\n          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">\n            {member.division}\n          </span>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className="relative w-full h-full overflow-hidden select-none"\n      onMouseEnter={() => setIsHovered(true)}\n      onMouseLeave={() => setIsHovered(false)}\n    >\n      {/* Stack of absolute images with smooth opacity & scale crossfade */}\n      {images.map((imgSrc, idx) => {\n        const isCurrent = idx === currentIdx;\n        const resolvedSrc = imgSrc.startsWith(\'http\') || imgSrc.startsWith(\'/\') ? `${basePath}${imgSrc}` : `${basePath}/${imgSrc}`;\n\n        return (\n          <img\n            key={`${imgSrc}-${idx}`}\n            src={resolvedSrc}\n            alt={`${member.name} - Foto ${idx + 1}`}\n            onError={() => onImageError && onImageError(member.id)}\n            className={`absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover object-center transition-all duration-1000 ease-in-out ${\n              isCurrent\n                ? \'opacity-100 scale-100 z-10 brightness-95 {images.length > 1 && (\n        <>\n          {/* Top-Right Multi-Photo Indicator Badge */}\n          <div className="absolute top-3.5 right-3.5 z-20 flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1 text-brand-orange animate-pulse" />\n            <span>\n              {currentIdx + 1}/{images.length}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Interactive Arrow Buttons on Modal or Hover */}\n          {(isModal || isHovered) && (\n            <>\n              <button\n                type="button"\n                onClick={prevSlide}\n                className="absolute </button>\n              <button\n                type="button"\n                onClick={nextSlide}\n                className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-20 w-7 h-7 sm:w-8 sm:h-8               aria-label="Foto berikutnya"\n              >\n                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />\n              </button>\n            </>\n          )}\n\n          {/* Bottom   <button\n                key={idx}\n                type="button"\n                onClick={(e) => {\n                  e.stopPropagation();\n                  setCurrentIdx(idx);\n                }}\n                className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer ${\n                  idx === currentIdx\n                    ? \'w-6 bg-brand-orange         ))}\n          </div>\n        </>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport const TeamRosterSection: React.FC<TeamRosterSectionProps> = ({\n  initialDivision = \'All\',\n  showHeader = document.body.style.overflow = \'unset\';\n      window.removeEventListener(\'keydown\', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [selectedMember]);\n\n  // Filter members\n  const isSearching =
